Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The acoustics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 958 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 806 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 958 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the acoustics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 806. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
